Injected wallets (MetaMask, Rabby, …)\n// work without it; the placeholder keeps getDefaultConfig from throwing when the\n// env var is missing so the app still builds and runs with injected wallets.\nconst projectId =\n  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLETCONNECT_PROJECT_ID ?? \"PLACEHOLDER_PROJECT_ID\";\n\nconst rpcUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL;\n\n// This app deals in the real USDC contract, so it is intentionally locked to\n// Ethereum mainnet. Anything else and we ask the user to switch.\nexport const config = getDefaultConfig({\n  appName: \"USDC Pay\",\n  projectId,\n  chains: [mainnet],\n  transports: {\n    // Falls back to the chain's public RPC when NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL is unset.\n    [mainnet.id]: http(rpcUrl),\n  },\n  ssr: true,\n});\n\ndeclare module \"wagmi\" {\n  interface Register {\n    config: typeof config;\n  }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZ4h1cF4d2eTJ1CCEEj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012BQJCNxhsthMUtwdtWmQH2","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T081614Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/lib/format.ts","content":"import { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\n\n/**\n * Format a raw token amount (base units) for display, grouping thousands and\n * capping the number of fractional digits shown without losing precision in the\n * underlying value.\n */\nexport function formatTokenAmount(\n  value: bigint,\n  decimals: number,\n  maxFractionDigits = 6,\n): string {\n  const formatted = formatUnits(value, decimals);\n  const [whole, fraction = \"\"] = formatted.split(\".\");\n  const groupedWhole = BigInt(whole).toLocaleString(\"en-US\");\n  const trimmedFraction = fraction.slice(0, maxFractionDigits).replace(/0+$/, \"\");\n  return trimmedFraction ? `${groupedWhole}.${trimmedFraction}` : groupedWhole;\n}\n\nexport type AmountParseResult =\n  | { ok: true; value: bigint }\n  | { ok: false; error: string };\n\n/**\n * Validate and parse a user-entered decimal amount string into base units.\n * Rejects malformed input and amounts with more fractional digits than the\n * token supports (USDC = 6), which would otherwise throw or silently truncate.\n */\nexport function parseAmount(\n  input: string,\n  decimals: number,\n): AmountParseResult {\n  const trimmed = input.trim();\n  if (!trimmed) return { ok: false, error: \"Enter an amount\" };\n\n  // A single well-formed decimal number, no signs or exponents.\n  if (!/^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(trimmed) || trimmed === \".\") {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"Enter a valid number\" };\n  }\n\n  const [, fraction = \"\"] = trimmed.split(\".\");\n  if (fraction.length > decimals) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      error: `USDC supports at most ${decimals} decimal places`,\n    };\n  }\n\n  let value: bigint;\n  try {\n    value = parseUnits(trimmed, decimals);\n  } catch {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"Enter a valid number\" };\n  }\n\n  if (value <= 0n) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"Amount must be greater than 0\" };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true, value };\n}\n\n/** Shorten an address for compact display: 0x1234…abcd. */\nexport function shortenAddress(address: string): string {\n  return `${address.slice(0, 6)}…${address.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3402,"cache_read_input_tokens":28207,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3402},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"da5e5639-20bf-4201-983e-f9833eb51a97","uuid":"0149bc09-1874-4b8f-a6e0-3b7e1080162c","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:18:38.212Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZ4fxMyXkF4GKS9hD9s"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZ6PxFMTTJerJJg9m2H","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DnptpCSPNLLymKxNypfZXZ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T081614Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/app/providers.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit/styles.css\";\n\nimport { RainbowKitProvider, lightTheme } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\nimport { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { useState, type ReactNode } from \"react\";\nimport { WagmiProvider } from \"wagmi\";\n\nimport { config } from \"@/lib/wagmi\";\n\nexport function Providers({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  // One QueryClient per browser session; created lazily so it is stable across\n  // re-renders but never shared between requests on the server.\n  const [queryClient] = useState(() => new QueryClient());\n\n  return (\n    <WagmiProvider config={config}>\n      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>\n        <RainbowKitProvider\n          theme={lightTheme({\n            accentColor: \"#2563eb\",\n            borderRadius: \"medium\",\n          })}\n        >\n          {children}\n        </RainbowKitProvider>\n      </QueryClientProvider>\n    </WagmiProvider>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2451,"cache_read_input_tokens":31609,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2451},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"da5e5639-20bf-4201-983e-f9833eb51a97","uuid":"d1682528-6f99-4d4d-b091-0da62846a16f","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:18:46.978Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZ6Na9cNYxm9xSRVHXR"}

First, the balances hook:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1842,"cache_read_input_tokens":34060,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1842},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"da5e5639-20bf-4201-983e-f9833eb51a97","uuid":"2b489a9b-7331-4996-8856-da91b22bbb14","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:19:28.864Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZ7RQLhtewx4Ay7J1jx"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZ7T5YaEeqUyuYCqEKc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018xoAuvfUpW1RpBrkJQ2gXr","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T081614Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/lib/useUsdcAccount.ts","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useAccount, useBalance, useReadContracts } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { mainnet } from \"wagmi/chains\";\n\nimport { USDC_ADDRESS, usdcAbi, USDC_DECIMALS_FALLBACK } from \"@/lib/usdc\";\n\nconst ZERO_ADDRESS = \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\" as const;\n\n/**\n * Everything the /pay page needs about the connected account: USDC metadata,\n * USDC balance, and ETH balance (for gas). Reads decimals/symbol from the token\n * itself rather than hardcoding, and refetches both balances together after a\n * successful transfer.\n */\nexport function useUsdcAccount() {\n  const { address, isConnected, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const onMainnet = chainId === mainnet.id;\n  const enabled = Boolean(address) && onMainnet;\n\n  const token = useReadContracts({\n    allowFailure: false,\n    contracts: [\n      { address: USDC_ADDRESS, abi: usdcAbi, functionName: \"decimals\" },\n      { address: USDC_ADDRESS, abi: usdcAbi, functionName: \"symbol\" },\n      {\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: usdcAbi,\n        functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n        args: [address ?? ZERO_ADDRESS],\n      },\n    ],\n    query: { enabled },\n  });\n\n  const eth = useBalance({ address, query: { enabled } });\n\n  const [decimals, symbol, usdcBalance] = token.data ?? [];\n\n  return {\n    address,\n    isConnected,\n    chainId,\n    onMainnet,\n    decimals: decimals ?? Reads decimals/symbol from the token\n * itself rather than hardcoding, and refetches both balances together after a\n * successful transfer.\n */\nexport function useUsdcAccount() {\n  const { address, isConnected, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const onMainnet = chainId === mainnet.id;\n  const enabled = Boolean(address) && onMainnet;\n\n  const token = useReadContracts({\n    allowFailure: false,\n    contracts: [\n      { address: USDC_ADDRESS, abi: usdcAbi, functionName: \"decimals\" },\n      { address: USDC_ADDRESS, abi: usdcAbi, functionName: \"symbol\" },\n      {\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: usdcAbi,\n        functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n        args: [address ?? ZERO_ADDRESS],\n      },\n    ],\n    query: { enabled },\n  });\n\n  const eth = useBalance({ address, query: { enabled } });\n\n  const [decimals, symbol, usdcBalance] = token.data ?? [];\n\n  return {\n    address,\n    isConnected,\n    chainId,\n    onMainnet,\n    decimals: decimals ?? USDC_DECIMALS_FALLBACK,\n    symbol: symbol ?? \"USDC\",\n    usdcBalance: usdcBalance as bigint | undefined,\n    ethBalance: eth.data,\n    isLoading: token.isLoading || eth.isLoading,\n    isError: token.isError || eth.isError,\n    refetch: () => {\n      void token.refetch();\n      void eth.refetch();\n    },\n  };\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZBSTCWbZNKHAvcod8k","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01SKkLV5ZamPg5Za5WusEqHh","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T081614Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/components/PayForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\nimport {\n  BaseError,\n  formatUnits,\n  getAddress,\n  isAddress,\n  type Address,\n} from \"viem\";\nimport { useWaitForTransactionReceipt, useWriteContract } from \"wagmi\";\n\nimport { formatTokenAmount, parseAmount, shortenAddress } from \"@/lib/format\";\nimport { USDC_ADDRESS, usdcAbi } from \"@/lib/usdc\";\n\ntype PayFormProps = {\n  owner: Address;\n  symbol: string;\n  decimals: number;\n  usdcBalance: bigint | undefined;\n  hasNoEth: boolean;\n  disabled?: boolean;\n  onConfirmed: () => void;\n};\n\nconst ETHERSCAN_TX = \"https://etherscan.io/tx/\";\n\nexport function PayForm({\n  owner,\n  symbol,\n  decimals,\n  usdcBalance,\n  hasNoEth,\n  disabled = false,\n  onConfirmed,\n}: PayFormProps) {\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [touched, setTouched] = useState(false);\n\n  const {\n    writeContract,\n    data: hash,\n    isPending,\n    error: writeError,\n    reset,\n  } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const {\n    isLoading: isConfirming,\n    isSuccess: isConfirmed,\n    error: receiptError,\n  } = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({ hash });\n\n  // Refetch balances and clear the form once the transfer confirms on-chain.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (isConfirmed) {\n      onConfirmed();\n      setRecipient(\"\");\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setTouched(false);\n    }\n  }, [isConfirmed, onConfirmed]);\n\n  const recipientValid = isAddress(recipient);\n  const amountResult = useMemo(\n    () => parseAmount(amount, decimals),\n    [amount, decimals],\n  );\n\n  const insufficient =\n    amountResult.ok &&\n    usdcBalance !== undefined &&\n    amountResult.value > usdcBalance;\n\n  const isSelf =\n    recipientValid &&\n    owner !== undefined &&\n    getAddress(recipient) === getAddress(owner);\n\n  const recipientError =\n    touched && recipient && !recipientValid\n      ? \"Enter a valid Ethereum address\"\n      : undefined;\n\n  const amountError = touched && amount && !amountResult.ok\n    ? amountResult.error\n    : insufficient\n      ? `Amount exceeds your ${symbol} balance`\n      : undefined;\n\n  const inFlight = isPending || isConfirming;\n  const canSubmit =\n    !disabled &&\n    !inFlight &&\n    recipientValid &&\n    amountResult.ok &&\n    !insufficient;\n\n  function handleAmountChange(raw: string) {\n    // Keep only digits and a single decimal point.\n    const cleaned = raw.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, \"\");\n    const firstDot = cleaned.indexOf(\".\");\n    const normalized =\n      firstDot === -1\n        ? cleaned\n        : cleaned.slice(0, firstDot + 1) +\n          cleaned.slice(firstDot + 1).replace(/\\./g, \"\");\n    setAmount(normalized);\n  }\n\n  function handleMax() {\n    if (usdcBalance !== undefined) {\n      setAmount(formatUnits(usdcBalance, decimals));\n    }\n  }\n\n  function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setTouched(true);\n    if (!recipientValid || !amountResult.ok || insufficient) return;\n\n    writeContract({\n      address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n      abi: usdcAbi,\n      functionName: \"transfer\",\n      args: [getAddress(recipient), amountResult.value],\n    });\n  }\n\n  const submitLabel = isPending\n    ? \"Confirm in wallet…\"\n    : isConfirming\n      ? \"Sending…\"\n      : `Send ${symbol}`;\n\n  const txError = writeError ?? receiptError;\n\n  return (\n    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"flex flex-col gap-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1.5\">\n        <label htmlFor=\"recipient\" className=\"text-sm font-medium text-slate-700\">\n          Recipient\n        </label>\n        <input\n          id=\"recipient\"\n          inputMode=\"text\"\n          autoComplete=\"off\"\n          spellCheck={false}\n          placeholder=\"0x…\"\n          value={recipient}\n          onChange={(e) => setRecipient(e.target.value.trim())}\n          onBlur={() => setTouched(true)}\n          disabled={disabled || inFlight}\n          className=\"w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 font-mono text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 disabled:bg-slate-50 disabled:text-slate-400\"\n        />\n        {recipientError ? (\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-red-600\">{recipientError}</p>\n        ) : isSelf ? (\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-amber-600\">\n            This is your own address — you&apos;d be sending to yourself.\n          </p>\n        ) : null}\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1.5\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n          <label htmlFor=\"amount\" className=\"text-sm font-medium text-slate-700\">\n            Amount\n          </label>\n          {usdcBalance !== undefined ? (\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              onClick={handleMax}\n              disabled={disabled || inFlight || usdcBalance === 0n}\n              className=\"text-xs font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700 disabled:text-slate-400\"\n            >\n              Max: {formatTokenAmount(usdcBalance, decimals, 2)} {symbol}\n            </button>\n          ) : null}\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"relative\">\n          <input\n            id=\"amount\"\n            inputMode=\"decimal\"\n            autoComplete=\"off\"\n            placeholder=\"0.00\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={(e) => handleAmountChange(e.target.value)}\n            onBlur={() => setTouched(true)}\n            disabled={disabled || inFlight}\n            className=\"w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 pr-16 text-sm tabular-nums outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 disabled:bg-slate-50 disabled:text-slate-400\"\n          />\n          <span className=\"pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-400\">\n            {symbol}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n        {amountError ? <p className=\"text-xs text-red-600\">{amountError}</p> : null}\n      </div>\n\n      {hasNoEth ? (\n        <p className=\"rounded-lg bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-700\">\n          You have no ETH to pay for gas. Add some ETH before sending.\n        </p>\n      ) : null}\n\n      <button\n        type=\"submit\"\n        disabled={!canSubmit}\n        className=\"mt-1 w-full rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300\"\n      >\n        {submitLabel}\n      </button>\n\n      {hash ? (\n        <div\n          className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-xs ${\n            isConfirmed\n              ? \"bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700\"\n              : \"bg-blue-50 text-blue-700\"\n          }`}\n        >\n          <p className=\"font-medium\">\n            {isConfirmed\n              ? \"Payment confirmed\"\n              : \"Transaction submitted — waiting for confirmation…\"}\n          </p>\n          <a\n            href={`${ETHERSCAN_TX}${hash}`}\n            target=\"_blank\"\n            rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n            className=\"font-mono underline underline-offset-2\"\n          >\n            {shortenAddress(hash)}\n          </a>\n        </div>\n      ) : null}\n\n      {txError ? (\n        <p className=\"rounded-lg bg-red-50 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-red-700\">\n          {txError instanceof BaseError\n            ? txError.shortMessage\n            : txError.message}\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            onClick={reset}\n            className=\"ml-2 font-medium underline\"\n          >\n            Dismiss\n          </button>\n        </p>\n      ) : null}\n    </form>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1070,"cache_read_input_tokens":39531,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1070},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"da5e5639-20bf-4201-983e-f9833eb51a97","uuid":"c52dd951-614b-48c0-a4c6-989f4d111f18","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:20:17.081Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZBR7anpRcjB4YCSxc7"}

\"Enter a valid Ethereum address\"\n      : undefined;\n\n  const amountError = touched && amount && !amountResult.ok\n    ? amountResult.error\n    : insufficient\n      ? `Amount exceeds your ${symbol} balance`\n      : undefined;\n\n  const inFlight = isPending || isConfirming;\n  const canSubmit =\n    !disabled &&\n    !inFlight &&\n    recipientValid &&\n    amountResult.ok &&\n    !insufficient;\n\n  function handleAmountChange(raw: string) {\n    // Keep only digits and a single decimal point.\n    const cleaned = raw.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, \"\");\n    const firstDot = cleaned.indexOf(\".\");\n    const normalized =\n      firstDot === -1\n        ? cleaned\n        : cleaned.slice(0, firstDot + 1) +\n          cleaned.slice(firstDot + 1).replace(/\\./g, \"\");\n    setAmount(normalized);\n  }\n\n  function handleMax() {\n    if (usdcBalance !== undefined) {\n      setAmount(formatUnits(usdcBalance, decimals));\n    }\n  }\n\n  function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setTouched(true);\n    if (!recipientValid || !amountResult.ok || insufficient) return;\n\n    writeContract({\n      address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n      abi: usdcAbi,\n      functionName: \"transfer\",\n      args: [getAddress(recipient), amountResult.value],\n    });\n  }\n\n  const submitLabel = isPending\n    ? \"Confirm in wallet…\"\n    : isConfirming\n      ? \"Sending…\"\n      : `Send ${symbol}`;\n\n  const txError = writeError ?? receiptError;\n\n  return (\n    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"flex flex-col gap-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1.5\">\n        <label htmlFor=\"recipient\" className=\"text-sm font-medium text-slate-700\">\n          Recipient\n        </label>\n        <input\n          id=\"recipient\"\n          inputMode=\"text\"\n          autoComplete=\"off\"\n          spellCheck={false}\n          placeholder=\"0x…\"\n          value={recipient}\n          onChange={(e) => setRecipient(e.target.value.trim())}\n          onBlur={() => setTouched(true)}\n          disabled={disabled || inFlight}\n          className=\"w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 font-mono text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 disabled:bg-slate-50 disabled:text-slate-400\"\n        />\n        {recipientError ? (\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-red-600\">{recipientError}</p>\n        ) : isSelf ? (\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-amber-600\">\n            This is your own address — you&apos;d be sending to yourself.\n          </p>\n        ) : null}\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1.5\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n          <label htmlFor=\"amount\" className=\"text-sm font-medium text-slate-700\">\n            Amount\n          </label>\n          {usdcBalance !== undefined ? (\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              onClick={handleMax}\n              disabled={disabled || inFlight || usdcBalance === 0n}\n              className=\"text-xs font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700 disabled:text-slate-400\"\n            >\n              Max: {formatTokenAmount(usdcBalance, decimals, 2)} {symbol}\n            </button>\n          ) : null}\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"relative\">\n          <input\n            id=\"amount\"\n            inputMode=\"decimal\"\n            autoComplete=\"off\"\n            placeholder=\"0.00\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={(e) => handleAmountChange(e.target.value)}\n            onBlur={() => setTouched(true)}\n            disabled={disabled || inFlight}\n            className=\"w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 pr-16 text-sm tabular-nums outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 disabled:bg-slate-50 disabled:text-slate-400\"\n          />\n          <span className=\"pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-400\">\n            {symbol}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n        {amountError ? <p className=\"text-xs text-red-600\">{amountError}</p> : null}\n      </div>\n\n      {hasNoEth ? (\n        <p className=\"rounded-lg bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-700\">\n          You have no ETH to pay for gas. Add some ETH before sending.\n        </p>\n      ) : null}\n\n      <button\n        type=\"submit\"\n        disabled={!canSubmit}\n        className=\"mt-1 w-full rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300\"\n      >\n        {submitLabel}\n      </button>\n\n      {hash ? (\n        <div\n          className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-xs ${\n            isConfirmed\n              ? \"bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700\"\n              : \"bg-blue-50 text-blue-700\"\n          }`}\n        >\n          <p className=\"font-medium\">\n            {isConfirmed\n              ? \"Payment confirmed\"\n              : \"Transaction submitted — waiting for confirmation…\"}\n          </p>\n          <a\n            href={`${ETHERSCAN_TX}${hash}`}\n            target=\"_blank\"\n            rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n            className=\"font-mono underline underline-offset-2\"\n          >\n            {shortenAddress(hash)}\n          </a>\n        </div>\n      ) : null}\n\n      {txError ? (\n        <p className=\"rounded-lg bg-red-50 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-red-700\">\n          {txError instanceof BaseError\n            ? txError.shortMessage\n            : txError.message}\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            onClick={reset}\n            className=\"ml-2 font-medium underline\"\n          >\n            Dismiss\n          </button>\n        </p>\n      ) : null}\n    </form>\n  );\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

(\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center gap-4 py-10 text-center\">\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-slate-600\">\n              Connect a wallet to view your balances and send USDC.\n            </p>\n            <ConnectButton\n              showBalance={false}\n              accountStatus=\"address\"\n              chainStatus=\"none\"\n              label=\"Connect wallet\"\n            />\n          </div>\n        ) : wrongNetwork ? (\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center gap-4 py-10 text-center\">\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-slate-600\">\n              This app uses USDC on Ethereum mainnet. Switch your network to\n              continue.\n            </p>\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: mainnet.id })}\n              disabled={isSwitching}\n              className=\"rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:bg-slate-300\"\n            >\n              {isSwitching ? \"Switching…\" : \"Switch to Ethereum\"}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-6\">\n            <Balances\n              symbol={account.symbol}\n              decimals={account.decimals}\n              usdcBalance={account.usdcBalance}\n              ethBalance={account.ethBalance}\n              isLoading={account.isLoading}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"h-px bg-slate-100\" />\n\n            <PayForm\n              owner={account.address!}\n              symbol={account.symbol}\n              decimals={account.decimals}\n              usdcBalance={account.usdcBalance}\n              hasNoEth={hasNoEth}\n              onConfirmed={account.refetch}\n            />\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </section>\n\n      <p className=\"mt-6 text-center text-xs text-slate-400\">\n        USDC · 0xA0b8…eB48 · Ethereum mainnet\n      </p>\n    </main>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3324,"cache_read_input_tokens":40601,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3324},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"da5e5639-20bf-4201-983e-f9833eb51a97","uuid":"31d50d15-db6d-4fb7-a663-8c3599cf0f54","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:20:32.249Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZDfFwg1Ry4Yn5FzfZu"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZEor9tbtht7D3VEKz4","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01QosmcNJwiShTXdj1Xa24ZS","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T081614Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Pay\n\nA minimal dApp for sending **USDC on Ethereum mainnet**. Connect a wallet, see\nyour USDC and ETH balances, enter a recipient and amount, and send.\n\n- **`/pay`** — the whole app. The root route redirects here.\n- USDC token: [`0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48`](https://etherscan.io/token/0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48)\n\n## Stack\n\n- **Next.js** (App Router) + **TypeScript**\n- **wagmi v2** + **viem v2** for chain reads/writes\n- **RainbowKit** for wallet connection\n- **TanStack Query** (wagmi's data layer)\n- **Tailwind CSS**\n\n## Getting started\n\n```bash\nnpm install\ncp .env.example .env.local   # add a WalletConnect project id\nnpm run dev                  # http://localhost:3000  → redirects to /pay\n```\n\n### Environment\n\n| Variable | Required | Notes |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLETCONNECT_PROJECT_ID` | For WalletConnect / mobile wallets | Free id from [Reown/WalletConnect Cloud](https://cloud.reown.com). Injected wallets (MetaMask, Rabby) work without it. |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL` | Recommended for production | A dedicated mainnet RPC (Alchemy/Infura). Falls back to a public RPC. |\n\n## How it works\n\n- **Balances** — reads `decimals`, `symbol` and `balanceOf` straight from the\n  USDC contract (batched via multicall) and the account's native ETH balance.\n  Both refetch automatically once a transfer confirms.\n- **Sending** — calls the ERC-20 `transfer(recipient, amount)` on USDC. The\n  amount is parsed with the token's real decimals.\n\n### USDC details handled faithfully\n\n- **6 decimals, not 18.** Amounts are parsed with `parseUnits(amount, 6)`, and\n  the input rejects more than 6 fractional digits. Decimals are read from the\n  contract at runtime rather than assumed.\n- **Checksummed address**, validated at module load via viem's `getAddress`.\n- **Mainnet-only.** The app is locked to chain id 1; on any other network it\n  prompts the user to switch before showing balances or the form.\n\n## Validation & UX\n\n- Recipient must be a valid Ethereum address (with a gentle warning when it's\n  your own address).\n- Amount must be a positive number within your USDC balance and within 6\n  decimals; a **Max** button fills your full balance.\n- ETH balance is shown because you need it for gas, with a warning when it's zero.\n- Full transaction lifecycle: *confirm in wallet → submitted → confirmed*, with\n  an Etherscan link and readable error messages (including user rejections).\n\n## Project structure\n\n```\napp/\n  layout.tsx        # root layout + providers\n  providers.tsx     # wagmi + RainbowKit + React Query\n  page.tsx          # redirects to /pay\n  pay/page.tsx      # the pay screen (connect, network guard, balances, form)\ncomponents/\n  Balances.tsx      # USDC + ETH balance tiles\n  PayForm.tsx       # recipient/amount form + send lifecycle\nlib/\n  wagmi.ts          # mainnet-only wagmi config\n  usdc.ts           # USDC address + ERC-20 ABI\n  useUsdcAccount.ts # balances + token metadata hook\n  format.ts         # amount parsing/formatting helpers\n```\n\n## Notes\n\n- Not audited; test with small amounts. ERC-20 transfers are irreversible — a\n  wrong recipient means lost funds. The app validates address format but cannot\n  verify you typed the *right* address.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1472,"cache_read_input_tokens":43925,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1472},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"da5e5639-20bf-4201-983e-f9833eb51a97","uuid":"2e729bde-d87b-4b63-9599-2a42e4175fab","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:20:59.653Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZEmsb5cX6ziYDdJsDi"}

Connect a wallet, see\nyour USDC and ETH balances, enter a recipient and amount, and send.\n\n- **`/pay`** — the whole app. The root route redirects here.\n- USDC token: [`0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48`](https://etherscan.io/token/0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48)\n\n## Stack\n\n- **Next.js** (App Router) + **TypeScript**\n- **wagmi v2** + **viem v2** for chain reads/writes\n- **RainbowKit** for wallet connection\n- **TanStack Query** (wagmi's data layer)\n- **Tailwind CSS**\n\n## Getting started\n\n```bash\nnpm install\ncp .env.example .env.local   # add a WalletConnect project id\nnpm run dev                  # http://localhost:3000  → redirects to /pay\n```\n\n### Environment\n\n| Variable | Required | Notes |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLETCONNECT_PROJECT_ID` | For WalletConnect / mobile wallets | Free id from [Reown/WalletConnect Cloud](https://cloud.reown.com). Injected wallets (MetaMask, Rabby) work without it. |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL` | Recommended for production | A dedicated mainnet RPC (Alchemy/Infura). Falls back to a public RPC. |\n\n## How it works\n\n- **Balances** — reads `decimals`, `symbol` and `balanceOf` straight from the\n  USDC contract (batched via multicall) and the account's native ETH balance.\n  Both refetch automatically once a transfer confirms.\n- **Sending** — calls the ERC-20 `transfer(recipient, amount)` on USDC. The\n  amount is parsed with the token's real decimals.\n\n### USDC details handled faithfully\n\n- **6 decimals, not 18.** Amounts are parsed with `parseUnits(amount, 6)`, and\n  the input rejects more than 6 fractional digits. Decimals are read from the\n  contract at runtime rather than assumed.\n- **Checksummed address**, validated at module load via viem's `getAddress`.\n- **Mainnet-only.** The app is locked to chain id 1; on any other network it\n  prompts the user to switch before showing balances or the form.\n\n## Validation & UX\n\n- Recipient must be a valid Ethereum address (with a gentle warning when it's\n  your own address).\n- Amount must be a positive number within your USDC balance and within 6\n  decimals; a **Max** button fills your full balance.\n- ETH balance is shown because you need it for gas, with a warning when it's zero.\n- Full transaction lifecycle: *confirm in wallet → submitted → confirmed*, with\n  an Etherscan link and readable error messages (including user rejections).\n\n## Project structure\n\n```\napp/\n  layout.tsx        # root layout + providers\n  providers.tsx     # wagmi + RainbowKit + React Query\n  page.tsx          # redirects to /pay\n  pay/page.tsx      # the pay screen (connect, network guard, balances, form)\ncomponents/\n  Balances.tsx      # USDC + ETH balance tiles\n  PayForm.tsx       # recipient/amount form + send lifecycle\nlib/\n  wagmi.ts          # mainnet-only wagmi config\n  usdc.ts           # USDC address + ERC-20 ABI\n  useUsdcAccount.ts # balances + token metadata hook\n  format.ts         # amount parsing/formatting helpers\n```\n\n## Notes\n\n- Not audited; test with small amounts. ERC-20 transfers are irreversible — a\n  wrong recipient means lost funds. The app validates address format but cannot\n  verify you typed the *right* address.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZYf6JZxNdH8v48D9YU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01G6Ah4u9biuCzenhesMQCgU","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T081614Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/next.config.mjs","content":"/** @type {import('next').NextConfig} */\nconst nextConfig = {\n  reactStrictMode: true,\n  webpack: (config, { webpack }) => {\n    // The Coinbase/Base-Account connector (pulled in transitively by\n    // RainbowKit's default wallet list) references the optional `@x402/*`\n    // payment-signing packages, which aren't published as resolvable modules.\n    // This app only does plain ERC-20 transfers and never touches that code\n    // path, so ignore the whole scope to let the bundle build.\n    config.plugins.push(\n      new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@x402\\// }),\n    );\n    // Silence optional pretty-logger / native deps some wallet SDKs reference.\n    config.externals.push(\"pino-pretty\", \"lokijs\", \"encoding\");\n    return config;\n  },\n};\n\nexport default nextConfig;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":670,"cache_read_input_tokens":52348,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":670},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"da5e5639-20bf-4201-983e-f9833eb51a97","uuid":"398fd08d-f294-401c-b2a2-0807e60fefee","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:25:00.794Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZYe8kaDEnFQcGCiHY5"}

Connect wallet,\nview USDC + ETH (gas) balances, send USDC via ERC-20 transfer.\n\n- USDC treated faithfully: 6 decimals read from contract, parseUnits(_, 6),\n  input rejects >6 fractional digits, checksummed mainnet address.\n- Mainnet-only with network-switch guard.\n- Full tx lifecycle (confirm -> submitted -> confirmed) w/ Etherscan link,\n  readable errors, balance refetch on confirm, Max button, no-ETH gas warning.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 <noreply@anthropic.com>\" && git log --oneline -1 && git status --short","description":"Commit the dApp"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1597,"cache_read_input_tokens":57896,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1597},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"da5e5639-20bf-4201-983e-f9833eb51a97","uuid":"6c046ae1-2afd-4462-ae1f-8959b2e4d24f","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:27:17.811Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZk7Xq7izKqNvN6ABd4"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZkiCAgFU3PxKaWd7k3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. Committed a finished, building dApp (working tree clean).\n\n## What I built\n\nA `/pay` dApp for sending **real USDC on Ethereum mainnet**, as its own committed git repo in the workspace.\n\n**Stack** (production-standard for an Ethereum dApp): Next.js App Router + TypeScript · wagmi v2 + viem v2 · RainbowKit · TanStack Query · Tailwind.\n\n**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` clean, `next build` exits 0 with zero warnings, `next lint` clean.\n\n### The `/pay` flow\nConnect wallet → see your **USDC balance** and **ETH balance** (labeled \"used for gas\") → enter recipient + amount → send. Root `/` redirects to `/pay`.\n\n### USDC treated faithfully\n- **6 decimals, not 18** — `parseUnits(amount, 6)`; the amount input rejects more than 6 fractional digits, and decimals/symbol are *read from the contract at runtime* rather than assumed.\n- Address checksummed and validated at load via viem's `getAddress`.\n- **Mainnet-only** (chain id 1): on any other network the UI blocks the form and offers a one-click switch.\n\n### Real-product details\n- Full tx lifecycle: *confirm in wallet → submitted → confirmed*, with an Etherscan link and readable errors (including user-rejection).\n- Recipient validation, insufficient-balance check, self-send warning, **Max** button, no-ETH-for-gas warning, and automatic balance refetch after a transfer confirms.\n\n### One notable fix\nRainbowKit's default wallet list transitively pulls the Coinbase Base-Account connector → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk`, whose optional `@x402/*` and RN-async-storage deps don't resolve in a browser build. Since this app only does a plain ERC-20 `transfer` and never touches that code path, I stubbed those modules in `next.config.mjs` (documented inline) rather than dragging in unused SDK surface.\n\n### Notes / assumptions\n- Committed as a **standalone repo** inside the workspace (parent is the evals repo) so the deliverable is self-contained.\n- `.env.example` documents `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLETCONNECT_PROJECT_ID` (needed only for WalletConnect/mobile wallets — injected wallets like MetaMask work without it) and an optional dedicated mainnet RPC. Committed a finished, building dApp (working tree clean).\n\n## What I built\n\nA `/pay` dApp for sending **real USDC on Ethereum mainnet**, as its own committed git repo in the workspace.\n\n**Stack** (production-standard for an Ethereum dApp): Next.js App Router + TypeScript · wagmi v2 + viem v2 · RainbowKit · TanStack Query · Tailwind.\n\n**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` clean, `next build` exits 0 with zero warnings, `next lint` clean.\n\n### The `/pay` flow\nConnect wallet → see your **USDC balance** and **ETH balance** (labeled \"used for gas\") → enter recipient + amount → send. Root `/` redirects to `/pay`.\n\n### USDC treated faithfully\n- **6 decimals, not 18** — `parseUnits(amount, 6)`; the amount input rejects more than 6 fractional digits, and decimals/symbol are *read from the contract at runtime* rather than assumed.\n- Address checksummed and validated at load via viem's `getAddress`.\n- **Mainnet-only** (chain id 1): on any other network the UI blocks the form and offers a one-click switch.\n\n### Real-product details\n- Full tx lifecycle: *confirm in wallet → submitted → confirmed*, with an Etherscan link and readable errors (including user-rejection).\n- Recipient validation, insufficient-balance check, self-send warning, **Max** button, no-ETH-for-gas warning, and automatic balance refetch after a transfer confirms.\n\n### One notable fix\nRainbowKit's default wallet list transitively pulls the Coinbase Base-Account connector → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk`, whose optional `@x402/*` and RN-async-storage deps don't resolve in a browser build. Since this app only does a plain ERC-20 `transfer` and never touches that code path, I stubbed those modules in `next.config.mjs` (documented inline) rather than dragging in unused SDK surface.\n\n### Notes / assumptions\n- Committed as a **standalone repo** inside the workspace (parent is the evals repo) so the deliverable is self-contained.\n- `.env.example` documents `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLETCONNECT_PROJECT_ID` (needed only for WalletConnect/mobile wallets — injected wallets like MetaMask work without it) and an optional dedicated mainnet RPC.
